	gsm_default = { VAR = gsm_ui_g_ind VALUE = 1 }

		count = 16
		limit = { OR = { any_country = { has_country_flag = gsm_in_grp exists = event_target:gsm_grp@this event_target:gsm_grp@this = { is_same_value = event_target:gsm_cur } check_variable = { which = gsm_ind value = root.gsm_ui_g_ind } } AND = { check_variable = { which = gsm_ui_g_ind value = 15 } NOT = { event_target:gsm_cur_home = { has_policy_flag = gsm_dout_on } } } AND = { check_variable = { which = gsm_ui_g_ind value = 16 } NOT = { event_target:gsm_cur_home = { has_policy_flag = gsm_dout_on } } } } }
		change_variable = { which = gsm_ui_g_ind value = 1 }
		if = { limit = { check_variable = { which = gsm_ui_g_ind value > 16 } } set_variable = { which = gsm_ui_g_ind value = 1 } }
	}
}

gsm_sig2 = {
	set_variable = { which = gsm_t_sx value = $VAR$ }
	if = { limit = { check_variable = { which = gsm_t_sx value < 0 } } multiply_variable = { which = gsm_t_sx value = -1 } }
	if = { limit = { check_variable = { which = gsm_t_sx value >= 1e+09 } } round_variable_to_closest = { which = $VAR$ value = 1e+08 } }
	else_if = { limit = { check_variable = { which = gsm_t_sx value >= 1e+08 } } round_variable_to_closest = { which = $VAR$ value = 1e+07 } }
	else_if = { limit = { check_variable = { which = gsm_t_sx value >= 1e+07 } } round_variable_to_closest = { which = $VAR$ value = 1e+06 } }
	else_if = { limit = { check_variable = { which = gsm_t_sx value >= 1e+06 } } round_variable_to_closest = { which = $VAR$ value = 100000 } }
	else_if = { limit = { check_variable = { which = gsm_t_sx value >= 100000 } } round_variable_to_closest = { which = $VAR$ value = 10000 } }
	else_if = { limit = { check_variable = { which = gsm_t_sx value >= 10000 } } round_variable_to_closest = { which = $VAR$ value = 1000 } }
	else_if = { limit = { check_variable = { which = gsm_t_sx value >= 1000 } } round_variable_to_closest = { which = $VAR$ value = 100 } }
	else_if = { limit = { check_variable = { which = gsm_t_sx value >= 100 } } round_variable_to_closest = { which = $VAR$ value = 10 } }
	else_if = { limit = { check_variable = { which = gsm_t_sx value >= 10 } } round_variable_to_closest = { which = $VAR$ value = 1 } }
	else_if = { limit = { check_variable = { which = gsm_t_sx value >= 1 } } round_variable_to_closest = { which = $VAR$ value = 0.1 } }
	else = { round_variable_to_closest = { which = $VAR$ value = 0.01 } }
}
gsm_ui_intel_tier = {
	set_variable = { which = gsm_t_itier value = 2 }
	set_variable = { which = gsm_t_ilv value = 4 }
	if = {
		limit = { event_target:gsm_t_icomp = { NOT = { has_country_flag = gsm_neutral } check_variable = { which = gsm_t_has_home value = 1 } } NOT = { is_same_value = event_target:gsm_t_home } }
		set_variable = { which = gsm_t_ilv value = 0 }
		if = { limit = { has_intel_level = { who = event_target:gsm_t_home category = economy level >= 1 } } set_variable = { which = gsm_t_ilv value = 1 } }
		if = { limit = { has_intel_level = { who = event_target:gsm_t_home category = economy level >= 2 } } set_variable = { which = gsm_t_ilv value = 2 } }
		if = { limit = { has_intel_level = { who = event_target:gsm_t_home category = economy level >= 3 } } set_variable = { which = gsm_t_ilv value = 3 } }
		if = { limit = { has_intel_level = { who = event_target:gsm_t_home category = economy level >= 4 } } set_variable = { which = gsm_t_ilv value = 4 } }
		set_variable = { which = gsm_t_itier value = 0 }
		if = { limit = { check_variable = { which = gsm_t_ilv value >= 1 } } set_variable = { which = gsm_t_itier value = 1 } }
		if = { limit = { check_variable = { which = gsm_t_ilv value >= 3 } } set_variable = { which = gsm_t_itier value = 2 } }
	}
}

gsm_gen_ui_directory = {
	set_variable = { which = gsm_ui_n_listed value = 0 }
	set_variable = { which = gsm_ui_n_private value = 0 }
	set_variable = { which = gsm_ui_n_distress value = 0 }
	set_variable = { which = gsm_ui_n_defunct value = 0 }
	set_variable = { which = gsm_ui_n_cls_1 value = 0 }
	set_variable = { which = gsm_ui_n_cls_2 value = 0 }
	set_variable = { which = gsm_ui_n_cls_3 value = 0 }
	set_variable = { which = gsm_ui_n_cls_4 value = 0 }
	set_variable = { which = gsm_ui_n_cls_5 value = 0 }
	set_variable = { which = gsm_ui_n_cls_6 value = 0 }
	set_variable = { which = gsm_ui_n_cls_7 value = 0 }
	set_variable = { which = gsm_ui_n_cls_8 value = 0 }
	set_variable = { which = gsm_ui_n_cls_9 value = 0 }
	every_country = {
		limit = { gsm_is_security = yes gsm_ui_visible = yes }
		if = { limit = { has_country_flag = gsm_defunct } root = { change_variable = { which = gsm_ui_n_defunct value = 1 } } }
		else_if = { limit = { has_country_flag = gsm_distress } root = { change_variable = { which = gsm_ui_n_distress value = 1 } } }
		else_if = { limit = { has_country_flag = gsm_listed } root = { change_variable = { which = gsm_ui_n_listed value = 1 } } }
		else = { root = { change_variable = { which = gsm_ui_n_private value = 1 } } }
		if = {
			limit = { NOT = { has_country_flag = gsm_defunct } }
			if = {
				limit = { check_variable = { which = gsm_class value = 1 } }
				root = { change_variable = { which = gsm_ui_n_cls_1 value = 1 } }
			}
			else_if = {
				limit = { check_variable = { which = gsm_class value = 2 } }
				root = { change_variable = { which = gsm_ui_n_cls_2 value = 1 } }
			}
			else_if = {
				limit = { check_variable = { which = gsm_class value = 3 } }
				root = { change_variable = { which = gsm_ui_n_cls_3 value = 1 } }
			}
			else_if = {
				limit = { check_variable = { which = gsm_class value = 4 } }
				root = { change_variable = { which = gsm_ui_n_cls_4 value = 1 } }
			}
			else_if = {
				limit = { check_variable = { which = gsm_class value = 5 } }
